HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B006","root_000945/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_27d872783c90a84ad210","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:1\",\"root_000737\",[\"B006\"]],[\"97:5:4\",\"root_000945\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Esenlik, önceki aşağı yönlü hareketi şafağın yükselişine bağlayan engelsiz bir geçit ya da çıkış yolu işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_288258532cbe41bf686a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:1\",\"root_000737\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Esenlik, önceki inişin savaşçı bir seferberlik olarak anlaşılmasını engeller ve onu barış taşıyan bir hareket olarak belirler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:7","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2e3023fe9371e06ac4e8","text":"7. **The enclosure splits and releases its contents.** Fixed **ف ج ر B001** supplies wide splitting and outflow. Across 97:1 and 97:4, *فِى لَيْلَةِ* and *فِيهَا* repeatedly locate the descended object and agents inside the night; *حَتَّىٰ* then carries that containment to *ٱلْفَجْر*. The packet states containment and boundary, while I infer that dawn's split releases the effects of what was deposited and ordered inside. Dawn becomes a productive opening, not merely the cessation of a special period.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003","root_001132/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_53528216d15b16c71b83","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:4\",\"root_000945\",[\"B003\"]],[\"97:5:5\",\"root_001132\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Şafağın açılması, daha önce soruyla yaklaşılan gizli geceyi görünür kılar; kapanış böylece hem geceyi hem bilme sürecini tamamlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B001","root_000737/B004","root_000823/B003","root_000945/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_54732b6f2f1d21ef5878","text":"1. **Peace as the protected duration of the descent.** Lexical evidence: `س ل م` B001, soundness/safety, and B004, peace opposite war; `ط ل ع` B001, rising-place/time; `ف ج ر` B002, dawn breaking from night. Activated by 97:4's descent of agents by permission and 97:3's contrast with possible `ش ه ر` B003 sword-display. The packet supplies peace, until, rising-place, dawn, and a previous authorized procession; I supply the temporal boundary arrow that the night is kept intact until the opening point. Reading change: \"سَلَامٌ\" reads as a maintained protected state spanning the whole interval, not only a static predicate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5ce5270337c721cea43c","text":"No later ayah exists in the packet, so no later-ayah surprise or correction emerged for 97:5.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63e2a4b567d23acbd24b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:5\",\"root_001132\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Şafak, gece içinde indirilen ve düzenlenen şeylerin etkilerini dışarı bırakan üretken bir yarılma ve akış noktasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6593ea5c5204b32529b5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:1\",\"root_000737\",[\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Esenlik, ilk indirmeden meleklerin inişine uzanan yetkili aktarımın eksiksiz teslim edildiği ve şafağa kadar korunduğu bir devir-teslim durumunu da anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6ab804316292da199227","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:4\",\"root_000945\",[\"B001\"]],[\"97:5:5\",\"root_001132\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Şafak sınırı yalnız bir saat değildir; ışığın yükselme eylemini ve gerçekleştiği eşiği de birlikte bildirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B001","root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6b714f9168ec9525c2a7","text":"3. **Dawn as rupture and outflow, not only brightness.** Lexical evidence: `ف ج ر` B001, wide splitting and outflow, and B002, dawn breaking from night; activated by `ر ح م` B003 from the basmalah, womb, `ل ي ل` B001, night, and `ق د ر` B001/B006, measured limit/fitting proportion. The packet supplies night, measured qadr, mercy/womb, and dawn; I supply the birth-like arrow that the measured enclosure splits open when its limit is reached. Reading change: \"ٱلْفَجْرِ\" becomes the moment of release from containment, not merely the clock-time ending the night.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73c9affaae8339e30988","text":"3. **A completed handover chain.** **س ل م B012** supplies handing something over so another receives it, consigning an affair, or relinquishing it. The prior sequence contains a causative delivery (97:1), descending agents, permission, and directives or affairs (97:4). The packet does not label those movements a transfer chain; I infer that *سَلَام* can name their successful handover state. This turns the night into an authorized relay in which what descends is received intact until dawn.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_747ccb6c476090449f15","text":"No later ayah remains after 97:5, so there was no subsequent activation to revise this section. The retrospective change came from rereading earlier ayat into the close: 97:1/97:4 made `س ل م` B012 a handover mechanism, 97:2 made `ط ل ع` B003 an answer to the knowledge-threshold, and the basmalah made `ف ج ر` B001/B002 feel like release from merciful containment rather than a bare time marker.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_77120fc45367a8a4f251","text":"5. **Dawn as act, time, and place of transition.** **ط ل ع B001** explicitly permits *مَطْلَع* to be the act, place, or time of a luminary's rising, and **ف ج ر B002** supplies dawn breaking from night. The preposition *حَتَّىٰ* makes this polyvalent word the limit without forcing one scope. No additional causal arrow is needed beyond taking all accepted scopes seriously: peace reaches the rising event, the rising time, and the threshold from which light rises. The endpoint is consequently dynamic and spatial as well as chronological.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000022/B004","root_000737/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7fb51d9246716ccbed84","text":"2. **A safe handover at the threshold.** Lexical evidence: `س ل م` B012, handing over and letting go; `ن ز ل` B002/B004 from 97:1/97:4, sending down and placing in order; `ء ذ ن` B004, authorization. The packet supplies descent, permission, and \"حتى مطلع\"; I supply the procedural arrow that what was sent down is safely handed over or released at the rising-point. Reading change: the closing line becomes a transfer scene: the night preserves and then delivers what entered it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003","root_000945/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a74dcc94de061bc63bfe","text":"4. **Disclosure after concealed knowing.** Lexical evidence: `ط ل ع` B003, overlooking/disclosing a matter, B006, access-point, and B009, visible appearance; activated by 97:2's `د ر ي` B001/B003, knowing and concealed pursuit. The packet supplies an earlier knowledge-question and the closing \"مطلع\"; I supply the epistemic arrow that the answer to \"what made you know?\" is completed by the opening/access point of dawn. Reading change: the final phrase reads as the visible disclosure of a reality previously approached only by question.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:8","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_aa9809171d0e7cd99232","text":"8. **Exploratory: fullness becoming generous overflow.** **ط ل ع B007** supplies fullness to the brim, and **ف ج ر B005** supplies overflowing generosity. The preceding *كُلِّ* activates **ك ل ل B003**, totality, while 97:3's **خ ي ر B005** and 97:0's **ر ح م B001** supply beneficence and mercy. I infer a fill-and-release mechanism: the night fills through comprehensive descent, then reaches an outflow threshold at dawn. The packet does not state that contents accumulate materially, which is the weak link; the activation nevertheless makes the final boundary the overflow of the night's good rather than its simple exhaustion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b032fc2c565236609cc2","text":"4. **A passage joining descent to ascent.** **س ل م B006** supplies a ladder, stair, or means leading to something, while fixed **ط ل ع B006** supplies ascent, overlook, or access point. These images reverse the two earlier descents at *مَطْلَعِ*, creating a down-then-up spatial sequence. I infer that the peaceful interval functions as the connecting passage between arrival from above and dawn's rising. The ayah thus gives *سَلَام* spatial work: it is also the unobstructed means through the night.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B006","root_000945/B006","root_001492/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c8d09a31f6248ff73fea","text":"5. **Peace as an ascent-means.** Lexical evidence: `س ل م` B006, ladder/means leading upward; `ط ل ع` B006, ascent/access-point; activated by `ن ز ل` B001/B002, descent. The packet supplies descending movement and a rising-point; I supply the reversal arrow that peace itself becomes the means by which the downward event is joined to upward emergence. Reading change: \"سَلَامٌ\" is not only absence of harm; it becomes the connective structure between descent and ascent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B003","root_001132/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ce747190dcdc3044aa67","text":"6. **The epistemic answer arrives as disclosure.** **ط ل ع B003** supplies overlooking, looking into, and disclosing a matter; **ف ج ر B002** supplies the break from night. These fixed branches answer 97:2's **د ر ي B001** question about being made to know, after 97:4 has introduced *كُلِّ أَمْرٍ*. I infer that dawn makes the concealed operation inspectable or manifest. The ayah therefore closes both a night and an inquiry: peace persists until disclosure opens.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e69acac0d981e6fbee86","text":"1. **Safety as the condition of the whole interval.** Fixed **س ل م B001** supplies soundness, safety, and freedom from harm or defect. The nominal predication *سَلَامٌ هِىَ* assigns that condition to the night, while *حَتَّىٰ* extends it to the dawn boundary. I infer that safety encompasses the interior activity described in 97:4 rather than naming only a greeting at one instant. The ayah reads as a continuous protected state in which descent, authorization, and every affair remain sound.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ea7285fd731805a3b7da","text":"2. **Peace corrects a possible martial descent.** **س ل م B004** supplies reconciliation, truce, and peace as the opposite of war. The earlier repeated root **ن ز ل** has an accepted **B007** image of parties coming down to fight; plural descending agents and commands in 97:4 can activate that martial possibility. The packet then explicitly supplies *سَلَام*; I infer that it blocks or reverses the hostile directional reading of the descent. The night is therefore a peace-bearing mobilization, not a battle deployment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000737/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f2935dcf67a7c68119fb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:1\",\"root_000737\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Esenlik, meleklerin inişini ve bütün işleri kuşatan, şafak sınırına dek kesintisiz süren bir korunmuşluk durumudur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B008","root_001132/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f8633240199d57a7846f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"97:5:4\",\"root_000945\",[\"B008\"]],[\"97:5:5\",\"root_001132\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gecenin bütün inişlerle dolup şafakta cömertçe taşması, son sınırı sürenin tükenmesi değil birikmiş iyiliğin dışarı akması gibi düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
